# TaxaID Ecosystem — Exported Function Parameter Audit

One row per parameter across every exported function (and traced internal helper) in all 9 TaxaID packages. Classification only (HARDCODED vs USER-DEFINED) -- no arbitrariness judgment; that is a later review pass.
